data-v-24ab1ab4=""><div class="index-item-title container" data-v-24ab1ab4=""><div class="subTitle clearfix tc" data-v-24ab1ab4="" data-v-700191c8=""><h3 data-v-24ab1ab4="" data-v-700191c8="">End-to-End Solutions</h3></div></div><div class="swiper index-swiper swiper-container-horizontal" data-v-191b247a="" data-v-24ab1ab4=""><div class="swiper-wrapper" data-v-191b247a="" style="transform: translate3d(-592.5px, 0px, 0px); transition-duration: 0ms;"><div class="swiper-slide swiper-slide-duplicate" data-swiper-slide-index="5" data-v-191b247a="" style="width: 375px; margin-right: 20px;"><a class="" data-v-191b247a="" href="/uk/solutions/solutionsByIndustry/201"><img class="swiper-lazy" data-src="https://www.dahuasecurity.com/asset/upload/solution/20171205/Energy.png" data-v-191b247a=""/><div class="solution-info" data-v-191b247a=""><h2 data-v-191b247a="">Critical Infrastructure</h2><div data-v-191b247a="">As a facility security manager, you need to be prepared for all risks including theft, natural disasters, terrorist attacks, and more, which could topple key infrastructures.</div><span class="iconfont icon-yuanjiantou" data-v-191b247a=""></span></div></a></div><div class="swiper-slide swiper-slide-duplicate swiper-slide-visible swiper-slide-prev" data-swiper-slide-index="6" data-v-191b247a="" style="width: 375px; margin-right: 20px;"><a class="" data-v-191b247a="" href="/uk/solutions/solutionsByIndustry/517"><img class="swiper-lazy swiper-lazy-loaded" data-v-191b247a="" src="https://www.dahuasecurity.com/asset/upload/solution/20180724/7502.png"/><div class="solution-info" data-v-191b247a=""><h2 data-v-191b247a="">Warehousing &amp; Logistics</h2><div data-v-191b247a="">With the fast development of modern logistic infrastructure, especially the fast growing of e-commerce industry, like Amazon, Alibaba etc, How to deliver the parcel efficiently and cost-effectively to end user has become a common challenge in the industry. All information you provide to us is stored on our secure servers behind firewalls. Any payment transactions will be encrypted using SSL technology. The safety and security of your information also depends on you. Where we have given you (or where you have chosen) a user name and password for access to certain parts of the Site, you are responsible for keeping these credentials confidential. We ask you not to share your credentials with anyone. We urge you to be careful about giving out information in public areas of the Site like message boards. The information you share in public areas may be viewed by any user of the Site. We periodically review the technologies used and policies implemented for securing the collected information.\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E \u003Cbr \u002F\u003E \u003Cp\u003E \tYour personal data will be retained for as long as required for the purposes described in this Privacy Policy or in so far as such is necessary for compliance with statutory obligations and for solving any disputes. \u003C\u002Fp\u003E To determine the appropriate retention period for personal data, we consider the amount, nature and sensitivity of the personal data, the potential risk of harm from unauthorised use or disclosure of your personal data, the purposes for which we process your personal data and whether we can achieve those purposes through other means, and the applicable legal requirements.\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E \u003Cbr \u002F\u003E \u003Cstrong\u003E6.International Transfers\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E We offer our Services through the use of servers located in different countries. If you are not able to find your personal data you may also send us an e-mail at DH_Privacy@dahuatech.com to request access to, or require us to correct or delete any personal data that you have provided to us. A summary of your legal rights is set out below: \u003C\u002Fp\u003E \u003Cp\u003E \t\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E \u003C\u002Fp\u003E \u003Cblockquote style=\"margin:0 0 0 40px;border:none;padding:0px;\"\u003E \t•Request access to your personal data (commonly known as a “\u003Cu\u003Edata subject access request\u003C\u002Fu\u003E”). This enables you to receive a copy of the personal data we hold about you and to check that we are lawfully processing it. \u003C\u002Fblockquote\u003E \u003Cblockquote style=\"margin:0 0 0 40px;border:none;padding:0px;\"\u003E \t\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E •Request correction of the personal data that we hold about you. This enables you to have any incomplete or inaccurate data we hold about you corrected, though we may need to verify the accuracy of the new data you provide to us. \u003C\u002Fblockquote\u003E \u003Cblockquote style=\"margin:0 0 0 40px;border:none;padding:0px;\"\u003E \t\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E •Request erasure of your personal data. This enables you to ask us to delete or remove personal data where there is no good reason for us continuing to process it. You also have the right to ask us to delete or remove your personal data where you have successfully exercised your right to object to processing (see below), where we may have processed your information unlawfully or where we are required to erase your personal data to comply with local law. Note, however, that we may not always be able to comply with your request of erasure for specific legal reasons which will be notified to you, if applicable, at the time of your request. \u003C\u002Fblockquote\u003E \u003Cblockquote style=\"margin:0 0 0 40px;border:none;padding:0px;\"\u003E \t\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E •Object to processing of your personal data where we are relying on a legitimate interest (or those of a third party) and there is something about your particular situation which makes you want to object to processing on this ground as you feel it impacts on your fundamental rights and freedoms. You also have the right to object where we are processing your personal data for direct marketing purposes. In some cases, we may demonstrate that we have compelling legitimate grounds to process your information which override your rights and freedoms. \u003C\u002Fblockquote\u003E \u003Cblockquote style=\"margin:0 0 0 40px;border:none;padding:0px;\"\u003E \t\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E •Request restriction of processing of your personal data. This enables you to ask us to suspend the processing of your personal data in the following scenarios: (a) if you want us to establish the data's accuracy; (b) where our use of the data is unlawful but you do not want us to erase it; (c) where you need us to hold the data even if we no longer require it as you need it to establish, exercise or defend legal claims; or (d) you have objected to our use of your data but we need to verify whether we have overriding legitimate grounds to use it. \u003C\u002Fblockquote\u003E \u003Cblockquote style=\"margin:0 0 0 40px;border:none;padding:0px;\"\u003E \t\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E •Request the transfer of your personal data to you or to a third party. We will provide to you, or a third party you have chosen, your personal data in a structured, commonly used, machine-readable format. Note that this right only applies to automated information which you initially provided consent for us to use or where we used the information to perform a contract with you. \u003C\u002Fblockquote\u003E \u003Cblockquote style=\"margin:0 0 0 40px;border:none;padding:0px;\"\u003E \t\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E \t\u003Cp\u003E \t\t•Withdraw consent at any time where we are relying on consent to process your personal data. However, this will not affect the lawfulness of any processing carried out before you withdraw your consent. If you withdraw your consent, we may not be able to provide certain products or services to you. We will advise you if this is the case at the time you withdraw your consent. \t\u003C\u002Fp\u003E \u003C\u002Fblockquote\u003E \u003Cp\u003E \t\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E \u003C\u002Fp\u003E For security reasons, we will take steps to authenticate your identity before providing access to your personal data.
